Experiments on large language models show that our method consistently outperforms existing SVD-based baselines across compression ratios, with the advantage becoming increasingly pronounced at aggressive compression budgets, where competing methods degrade substantially or collapse entirely, offering a practical solution for efficient, large-scale model deployment.

</details>


### [150] [Auction-Based Online Policy Adaptation for Evolving Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02151)
*Guruprerana Shabadi,Kaushik Mallik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于拍卖机制的模块化多目标强化学习框架，通过自私局部策略竞标执行权，动态适应运行时目标变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多目标强化学习中目标在运行时动态出现或消失的问题，需要设计能够高效适应目标集变化的策略

Method: 模块化框架：每个目标对应自私局部策略，通过拍卖机制协调执行权（策略竞标反映状态紧急程度）；目标变化时只需增删对应策略；将问题转化为一般和博弈，策略需优化自身目标并学习产生校准竞标；使用PPO并发训练策略

Result: 在Atari Assault和网格世界路径规划任务（动态目标）上评估，相比PPO训练的单一策略获得显著更好的性能

Conclusion: 拍卖机制实现了目标间的动态可解释权衡，模块化设计支持运行时即时适应，相同策略族可参数化部署

Abstract: We consider multi-objective reinforcement learning problems where objectives come from an identical family -- such as the class of reachability objectives -- and may appear or disappear at runtime. Our goal is to design adaptive policies that can efficiently adjust their behaviors as the set of active objectives changes. To solve this problem, we propose a modular framework where each objective is supported by a selfish local policy, and coordination is achieved through a novel auction-based mechanism: policies bid for the right to execute their actions, with bids reflecting the urgency of the current state. The highest bidder selects the action, enabling a dynamic and interpretable trade-off among objectives. Going back to the original adaptation problem, when objectives change, the system adapts by simply adding or removing the corresponding policies. Moreover, as objectives arise from the same family, identical copies of a parameterized policy can be deployed, facilitating immediate adaptation at runtime. We show how the selfish local policies can be computed by turning the problem into a general-sum game, where the policies compete against each other to fulfill their own objectives. To succeed, each policy must not only optimize its own objective, but also reason about the presence of other goals and learn to produce calibrated bids that reflect relative priority. In our implementation, the policies are trained concurrently using proximal policy optimization (PPO). We evaluate on Atari Assault and a gridworld-based path-planning task with dynamic targets. Our method achieves substantially better performance than monolithic policies trained with PPO.

</details>


### [151] [Smoothing the Landscape: Causal Structure Learning via Diffusion Denoising Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02250)
*Hao Zhu,Di Zhou,Donna Slonim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDCD利用扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标来平滑梯度，实现更快更稳定的收敛，并提出自适应k跳无环约束以提升运行效率，用于从观测数据中发现因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如NOTEARS、DAG-GNN）在高维数据中面临可扩展性和稳定性问题，特别是在特征-样本不平衡的情况下。需要更稳定、更高效的因果发现框架。

Method: 提出Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD)框架：1) 利用扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标平滑梯度，实现更快更稳定的收敛；2) 提出自适应k跳无环约束，避免矩阵求逆，提升运行效率；3) 不同于生成式扩散模型，DDCD利用反向去噪过程推断参数化因果结构而非生成数据。

Result: 在合成基准数据上展示了DDCD的竞争性能；通过两个真实世界案例的定性分析证明了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: DDCD通过扩散模型的去噪分数匹配和自适应无环约束，为高维数据中的因果发现提供了更稳定、更高效的解决方案，在合成和真实数据上都表现出良好性能。

Abstract: Understanding causal dependencies in observational data is critical for informing decision-making. These relationships are often modeled as Bayesian Networks (BNs) and Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs). Existing methods, such as NOTEARS and DAG-GNN, often face issues with scalability and stability in high-dimensional data, especially when there is a feature-sample imbalance. Here, we show that the denoising score matching objective of diffusion models could smooth the gradients for faster, more stable convergence. We also propose an adaptive k-hop acyclicity constraint that improves runtime over existing solutions that require matrix inversion. We name this framework Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD). Unlike generative diffusion models, DDCD utilizes the reverse denoising process to infer a parameterized causal structure rather than to generate data. We demonstrate the competitive performance of DDCDs on synthetic benchmarking data. We also show that our methods are practically useful by conducting qualitative analyses on two real-world examples. Code is available at this url: https://github.com/haozhu233/ddcd.

</details>


### [152] [Unifying Group-Relative and Self-Distillation Policy Optimization via Sample Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02288)
*Gengsheng Li,Tianyu Yang,Junfeng Fang,Mingyang Song,Mao Zheng,Haiyun Guo,Dan Zhang,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SRPO结合GRPO和SDPO优势，通过样本路由机制将正确样本导向GRPO的奖励对齐强化学习，将失败样本导向SDPO的针对性logit级修正，并引入熵感知动态加权机制，实现快速早期改进和长期稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法存在局限性：GRPO采用粗粒度信用分配，对失败rollout进行统一惩罚，缺乏token级针对性；SDPO提供更密集的logit级监督，能实现快速早期改进，但在长期训练中经常崩溃。需要解决SDPO晚期不稳定的内在缺陷。

Method: 提出SRPO框架：1) 样本路由机制：正确样本使用GRPO的奖励对齐强化学习，失败样本使用SDPO的针对性logit级修正；2) 熵感知动态加权：抑制高熵不可靠蒸馏目标，强调置信度高的目标；3) 统一on-policy框架。

Result: 在5个基准测试和2个模型规模上评估：SRPO同时实现了SDPO的快速早期改进和GRPO的长期稳定性，持续超越两个基线的峰值性能，在Qwen3-8B上将5个基准平均性能提升3.4%（相比GRPO）和6.3%（相比SDPO），同时产生适中的响应长度，并将每步计算成本降低高达17.2%。

Conclusion: SRPO通过巧妙结合GRPO和SDPO的优势，解决了SDPO晚期不稳定的问题，实现了快速收敛和长期稳定训练的统一框架，在性能、效率和稳定性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for post-training large language models. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely adopted, its coarse credit assignment uniformly penalizes failed rollouts, lacking the token-level focus needed to efficiently address specific deviations. Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) addresses this by providing denser, more targeted logit-level supervision that facilitates rapid early improvement, yet it frequently collapses during prolonged training. We trace this late-stage instability to two intrinsic flaws: self-distillation on already-correct samples introduces optimization ambiguity, and the self-teacher's signal reliability progressively degrades. To resolve these issues, we propose Sample-Routed Policy Optimization (SRPO), a unified on-policy framework that routes correct samples to GRPO's reward-aligned reinforcement and failed samples to SDPO's targeted logit-level correction. SRPO further incorporates an entropy-aware dynamic weighting mechanism to suppress high-entropy, unreliable distillation targets while emphasizing confident ones. Evaluated across five benchmarks and two model scales, SRPO achieves both the rapid early improvement of SDPO and the long-horizon stability of GRPO. It consistently surpasses the peak performance of both baselines, raising the five-benchmark average on Qwen3-8B by 3.4% over GRPO and 6.3% over SDPO, while simultaneously yielding moderate response lengths and lowering per-step compute cost by up to 17.2%.

</details>


### [153] [Taming the Exponential: A Fast Softmax Surrogate for Integer-Native Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02292)
*Dimitrios Danopoulos,Enrico Lupi,Michael Kagan,Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HCCS（Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax）作为softmax的替代方案，针对AMD Versal AI Engines优化，使用int8 MAC单元实现高性能推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低精度推理的小型Transformer模型中，softmax成为计算瓶颈，指数运算和归一化带来显著开销。AMD AI Engine的现有实现（bfloat16或LUT）无法充分利用其高吞吐量整数向量处理单元

Method: 提出HCCS：基于最大中心化注意力logits的裁剪线性映射，包含轻量级校准参数，针对每个注意力头离线优化以保持统计特性。针对AMD AI Engine的int8 MAC单元进行硬件优化实现

Result: HCCS在AMD AI Engine上显著超越其他参考实现的速度性能，在小型或重度量化MHA工作负载上保持竞争性任务精度（经过量化感知重训练）

Conclusion: HCCS是首个针对AMD AI Engine的int8优化softmax替代方案，有效解决softmax计算瓶颈，充分利用硬件特性实现高性能推理

Abstract: Softmax can become a computational bottleneck in the Transformer model's Multi-Head Attention (MHA) block, particularly in small models under low-precision inference, where exponentiation and normalization incur significant overhead. As such, we suggest using Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS), a bounded, monotone surrogate to the exponential softmax function, which uses a clipped linear mapping of the max centered attention logits. This approximation produces a stable probability distribution, maintains the ordering of the original logits and has non-negative values. HCCS differs from previous softmax surrogates as it includes a set of lightweight calibration parameters that are optimized offline based on a representative dataset and calibrated for each individual attention head to preserve the statistical properties of the individual heads. We describe a hardware-motivated implementation of HCCS for high-throughput scenarios targeting the AMD Versal AI Engines. The current reference implementations from AMD for this platform rely upon either bfloat16 arithmetic or LUTs to perform the exponential operation, which might limit the throughput of the platform and fail to utilize the high-throughput integer vector processing units of the AI Engine. In contrast, HCCS provides a natural mapping to the AI Engines' int8 multiply accumulate (MAC) units. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first int8 optimized softmax surrogate for AMD AI engines that significantly exceeds the speed performance of other reference implementations while maintaining competitive task accuracy on small or heavily quantized MHA workloads after quantization-aware retraining.

</details>


### [154] [go-$m$HC: Direct Parameterization of Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections via Generalized Orthostochastic Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02309)
*Torque Dandachi,Sophia Diggs-Galligan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出go-mHC方法，通过广义正交随机矩阵理论实现双随机矩阵的精确参数化，以O(d³)复杂度在计算效率和完全表达能力之间连续插值，显著提升流混合任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双随机矩阵能够实现残差流之间的学习混合，但精确高效地参数化双随机矩阵集合（Birkhoff多面体）仍是一个开放挑战。现有精确方法随流数量d呈阶乘级扩展，而Kronecker分解方法虽高效但表达能力有限。

Method: 基于广义正交随机矩阵理论提出新颖的精确参数化方法，复杂度为O(d³)，通过超参数s在计算高效边界和完全表达Birkhoff多面体之间连续插值。该方法在流形约束超连接(mHC)框架上实例化为go-mHC，可与Kronecker分解方法自然组合，以相似的FLOP成本大幅恢复表达能力。

Result: 谱分析表明go-mHC比Kronecker分解基线更完整地填充Birkhoff多面体。在合成流混合任务中，go-mHC达到最小理论损失，收敛速度提升高达10倍。在3000万参数的GPT风格语言模型中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: go-mHC的表达能力、效率和精确性为将d作为模型容量的新维度进行扩展提供了实用途径，解决了双随机矩阵参数化的核心挑战。

Abstract: Doubly stochastic matrices enable learned mixing across residual streams, but parameterizing the set of doubly stochastic matrices (the Birkhoff polytope) exactly and efficiently remains an open challenge. Existing exact methods scale factorially with the number of streams ($d$), while Kronecker-factorized approaches are efficient but expressivity-limited. We introduce a novel exact parameterization grounded in the theory of generalized orthostochastic matrices, which scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ and exposes a single hyperparameter $s$ which continuously interpolates between a computationally efficient boundary and the fully expressive Birkhoff polytope. Building on Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections ($m$HC), a framework for learned dynamic layer connectivity, we instantiate this parameterization in go-$m$HC. Our method composes naturally with Kronecker-factorized methods, substantially recovering expressivity at similar FLOP costs. Spectral analysis indicates that go-$m$HC fills the Birkhoff polytope far more completely than Kronecker-factorized baselines. On synthetic stream-mixing tasks, go-$m$HC achieves the minimum theoretical loss while converging up to $10\times$ faster. We validate our approach in a 30M parameter GPT-style language model. The expressivity, efficiency, and exactness of go-$m$HC offer a practical avenue for scaling $d$ as a new dimension of model capacity.

</details>


### [155] [Batched Contextual Reinforcement: A Task-Scaling Law for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02322)
*Bangji Yang,Hongbo Ma,Jiajun Fan,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BCR是一种通过批量上下文训练实现高效推理的单阶段方法，在保持或提升准确率的同时显著减少token消耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有链式思维推理方法token消耗过高，而现有效率方法要么降低推理质量，要么需要复杂训练流程，需要一种简单有效的解决方案

Method: 批量上下文强化学习：在共享上下文窗口中同时训练模型解决N个问题，仅基于每个实例的准确率进行奖励，创建隐式token预算约束

Result: 1) 发现任务缩放规律：并发问题数N增加时，每个问题的token使用单调减少而准确率下降更平缓；2) 在标准单问题推理中实现"免费午餐"：token使用减少15.8%-62.6%同时保持或提升准确率；3) 模型自主消除冗余元认知循环；4) 避免显式长度惩罚的对抗梯度和灾难性优化崩溃

Conclusion: BCR通过简单的结构激励解锁了LLM中的潜在高密度推理能力，提供了一种高度稳定、基于约束的长度控制替代方案

Abstract: Large Language Models employing Chain-of-Thought reasoning achieve strong performance but suffer from excessive token consumption that inflates inference costs. Existing efficiency methods such as explicit length penalties, difficulty estimators, or multi-stage curricula either degrade reasoning quality or require complex training pipelines. We introduce Batched Contextual Reinforcement, a minimalist, single-stage training paradigm that unlocks efficient reasoning through a simple structural modification: training the model to solve N problems simultaneously within a shared context window, rewarded purely by per-instance accuracy. This formulation creates an implicit token budget that yields several key findings: (1) We identify a novel task-scaling law: as the number of concurrent problems N increases during inference, per-problem token usage decreases monotonically while accuracy degrades far more gracefully than baselines, establishing N as a controllable throughput dimension. (2) BCR challenges the traditional accuracy-efficiency trade-off by demonstrating a "free lunch" phenomenon at standard single-problem inference. Across both 1.5B and 4B model families, BCR reduces token usage by 15.8% to 62.6% while consistently maintaining or improving accuracy across five major mathematical benchmarks. (3) Qualitative analyses reveal emergent self-regulated efficiency, where models autonomously eliminate redundant metacognitive loops without explicit length supervision. (4) Crucially, we empirically demonstrate that implicit budget constraints successfully circumvent the adversarial gradients and catastrophic optimization collapse inherent to explicit length penalties, offering a highly stable, constraint-based alternative for length control. These results prove BCR practical, showing simple structural incentives unlock latent high-density reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Bench2Drive-VL: Benchmarks for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01259)
*Xiaosong Jia,Yuqian Shao,Zhenjie Yang,Qifeng Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bench2Drive-VL：首个面向视觉语言模型的自动驾驶闭环评估基准，通过自动生成多样化、行为驱动的问答对，在CARLA仿真环境中评估VLM在累积误差和分布外输入下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM4AD基准仅通过静态问答数据集进行开环评估，无法评估模型在累积误差和分布外状态下的性能。自动驾驶需要更可靠的闭环评估方法，但当前缺乏针对视觉语言模型的闭环基准。

Method: 1. DriveCommenter：闭环生成器，自动为CARLA中所有驾驶场景生成多样化、行为驱动的问答对，包括严重偏离路线和越野等罕见情况；2. 统一协议和接口：允许现代VLM直接接入Bench2Drive闭环环境与传统智能体比较；3. 灵活推理与控制框架：支持多格式视觉输入和可配置的基于图的思维链执行；4. 完整开发生态系统。

Result: 开发了Bench2Drive-VL基准，包含自动生成的多样化问答数据集、统一接口和完整开发工具。所有代码和标注数据集均已开源。

Conclusion: Bench2Drive-VL填补了VLM4AD领域闭环评估的空白，通过自动生成多样化场景的问答对，能够更可靠地评估视觉语言模型在自动驾驶任务中的实际性能，特别是在累积误差和分布外输入下的表现。

Abstract: With the rise of vision-language models (VLM), their application for autonomous driving (VLM4AD) has gained significant attention. Meanwhile, in autonomous driving, closed-loop evaluation has become widely recognized as a more reliable validation method than open-loop evaluation, as it can evaluate the performance of the model under cumulative errors and out-of-distribution inputs. However, existing VLM4AD benchmarks evaluate the model`s scene understanding ability under open-loop, i.e., via static question-answer (QA) dataset. This kind of evaluation fails to assess the VLMs performance under out-of-distribution states rarely appeared in the human collected datasets.To this end, we present Bench2Drive-VL, an extension of Bench2Drive that brings closed-loop evaluation to VLM-based driving, which introduces: (1) DriveCommenter, a closed-loop generator that automatically generates diverse, behavior-grounded question-answer pairs for all driving situations in CARLA,including severe off-route and off-road deviations previously unassessable in simulation. (2) A unified protocol and interface that allows modern VLMs to be directly plugged into the Bench2Drive closed-loop environment to compare with traditional agents. (3) A flexible reasoning and control framework, supporting multi-format visual inputs and configurable graph-based chain-of-thought execution. (4) A complete development ecosystem. Together, these components form a comprehensive closed-loop benchmark for VLM4AD. All codes and annotated datasets are open sourced.

</details>


### [157] [Open-loop POMDP Simplification and Safe Skipping of Replanning with Formal Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01352)
*Da Kong,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具有形式性能保证的自适应开环简化框架，通过拓扑信念树在开环和闭环规划间自适应切换，显著降低POMDP规划复杂度，并提供可计算边界确保识别原始POMDP的最优动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP为不确定性下的决策提供了原则性数学框架，但精确求解计算上不可行。现有方法在计算复杂性和性能保证之间存在权衡，需要一种既能降低计算复杂度又能提供形式性能保证的解决方案。

Method: 提出自适应开环简化框架，通过拓扑信念树在开环和闭环规划间自适应切换。推导出可高效计算的边界，确保简化框架能识别原始POMDP的最优动作。开发了采样求解器和随时求解器两种实用在线求解器。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在保持可证明性能保证的同时实现了显著的计算加速。提出的框架首次解决了具有形式性能保证的重新规划跳过问题，并提供了多步开环动作序列的理论保证。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应开环简化显著提高了POMDP规划的可处理性和效率，在降低计算复杂度的同时提供了形式性能保证，推进了POMDP规划的实际应用。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a principled mathematical framework for decision-making under uncertainty. However, the exact solution to POMDPs is computationally intractable. In this paper, we address the computational intractability by introducing a novel framework for adaptive open-loop simplification with formal performance guarantees. Our method adaptively interleaves open-loop and closed-loop planning via a topology-based belief tree, enabling a significant reduction in planning complexity. The key contribution lies in the derivation of efficiently computable bounds which provide formal guarantees and can be used to ensure that our simplification can identify the immediate optimal action of the original POMDP problem. Our framework therefore provides computationally tractable performance guarantees for macro-actions within POMDPs. Furthermore, we propose a novel framework for safely skipping replanning during execution, supported by theoretical guarantees on multi-step open-loop action sequences. To the best of our knowledge, this framework is the first to address skipping replanning with formal performance guarantees. Practical online solvers for our proposed simplification are developed, including a sampling-based solver and an anytime solver. Empirical results demonstrate substantial computational speedups while maintaining provable performance guarantees, advancing the tractability and efficiency of POMDP planning.

</details>


### [158] [A soft and lightweight fabric-based pneumatic interface for multimodal fingertip tactile feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01390)
*Rui Chen,Daniele Leonardis,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于织物的气动驱动指尖触觉设备，重量仅2.1克，通过手腕控制单元实现无线操作，支持多种触觉模式，分类准确率超过90%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴指尖触觉设备难以同时实现充分的触觉输出、低质量、简单制造和无束缚便携性，需要一种能解决这些挑战的新技术方案

Method: 采用基于织物的气动驱动技术，通过计算机数控热封工艺制造热塑性聚氨酯涂层织物制成的四个气动腔，形成柔软、贴合指尖的接口，配合手腕安装的控制单元实现无线操作

Result: 设备重量仅2.1克，机械和动态特性测试确认织物驱动器能产生足够的力、位移和带宽用于指尖触觉渲染；15名参与者的心理物理学研究显示，在三种触觉模式（接触配置、定向滑动和振动触觉频率）上的分类准确率超过90%

Conclusion: 基于织物的气动驱动技术为轻量、低成本、多模态的指尖触觉接口提供了一条可行的技术路线，解决了现有设备在触觉输出、重量、制造和便携性方面的挑战

Abstract: Wearable fingertip haptic devices are critical for realistic interaction in virtual reality, augmented reality, and teleoperation, yet existing approaches struggle to simultaneously achieve adequate tactile output, low mass, simple fabrication, and untethered portability. Here we show that fabric-based pneumatic actuation can address this gap. Our device comprises four pneumatic chambers fabricated from thermoplastic polyurethane-coated fabric via computer numerical control heat-sealing, yielding a soft, conformable interface weighing 2.1 g that operates untethered with a wrist-mounted control unit. Mechanical and dynamic characterization confirms that the fabric actuators produce sufficient force, displacement, and bandwidth for fingertip tactile rendering. A psychophysical study with 15 participants demonstrates classification accuracy exceeding 90% across three distinct tactile modes -- contact configuration, directional sliding, and vibrotactile frequency. These findings establish fabric-based pneumatic actuation as a viable technology route for lightweight, low-cost, and multimodal fingertip haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [159] [Learning When to See and When to Feel: Adaptive Vision-Torque Fusion for Contact-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01414)
*Jiuzhou Lei,Chang Liu,Yu She,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究比较了不同力/力矩-视觉融合策略在基于扩散的机器人操作策略中的表现，并提出了一种自适应融合方法，在非接触阶段忽略力/力矩信号，在接触阶段自适应利用视觉和力矩信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉策略在机器人操作中表现良好，但在接触密集和力敏感任务中，纯视觉感知不足，需要力/力矩信号提供接触动力学、对齐和交互质量的关键信息。尽管已有多种视觉与力/力矩信号融合策略，但缺乏对这些方法的系统比较。

Method: 研究比较了不同的力/力矩-视觉融合策略，包括辅助预测目标、专家混合架构和接触感知门控机制。提出了一种自适应融合策略，在非接触阶段忽略力/力矩信号，在接触阶段自适应地融合视觉和力矩信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的自适应融合方法在成功率上比最强基线提高了14%，突显了接触感知多模态融合对机器人操作的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究系统比较了不同的力/力矩-视觉融合策略，提出的自适应融合方法在基于扩散的操作策略中表现出色，验证了接触感知多模态融合对提升机器人操作性能的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-based policies have achieved a good performance in robotic manipulation due to the accessibility and richness of visual observations. However, purely visual sensing becomes insufficient in contact-rich and force-sensitive tasks where force/torque (F/T) signals provide critical information about contact dynamics, alignment, and interaction quality. Although various strategies have been proposed to integrate vision and F/T signals, including auxiliary prediction objectives, mixture-of-experts architectures, and contact-aware gating mechanisms, a comparison of these approaches remains lacking. In this work, we provide a comparison study of different F/T-vision integration strategies within diffusion-based manipulation policies. In addition, we propose an adaptive integration strategy that ignores F/T signals during non-contact phases while adaptively leveraging both vision and torque information during contact. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms the strongest baseline by 14% in success rate, highlighting the importance of contact-aware multimodal fusion for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [160] [Low-Burden LLM-Based Preference Learning: Personalizing Assistive Robots from Natural Language Feedback for Users with Paralysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01463)
*Keshav Shankar,Dan Ding,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个低负担的离线框架，利用大型语言模型将自然语言反馈直接转换为确定性机器人控制策略，显著减轻重度运动障碍用户的认知负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好学习方法（如详尽成对比较）会给重度运动障碍用户带来严重的身体和认知疲劳，需要一种低负担的方法来个性化物理辅助机器人的行为

Method: 提出离线框架，使用基于职业治疗实践框架（OTPF）的大型语言模型，将非结构化自然语言反馈解码为明确的生理和心理需求，然后映射为透明决策树，并通过"LLM-as-a-Judge"自动验证代码结构安全性

Result: 在10名瘫痪成人的模拟备餐研究中验证，自然语言方法显著降低了用户工作负荷，独立临床专家确认生成策略安全且准确反映用户偏好

Conclusion: 该框架成功将自然语言反馈转化为安全的机器人控制策略，为物理辅助机器人提供了一种低负担的个性化方法，解决了传统偏好学习方法的疲劳问题

Abstract: Physically Assistive Robots (PARs) require personalized behaviors to ensure user safety and comfort. However, traditional preference learning methods, like exhaustive pairwise comparisons, cause severe physical and cognitive fatigue for users with profound motor impairments. To solve this, we propose a low-burden, offline framework that translates unstructured natural language feedback directly into deterministic robotic control policies. To safely bridge the gap between ambiguous human speech and robotic code, our pipeline uses Large Language Models (LLMs) grounded in the Occupational Therapy Practice Framework (OTPF). This clinical reasoning decodes subjective user reactions into explicit physical and psychological needs, which are then mapped into transparent decision trees. Before deployment, an automated "LLM-as-a-Judge" verifies the code's structural safety. We validated this system in a simulated meal preparation study with 10 adults with paralysis. Results show our natural language approach significantly reduces user workload compared to traditional baselines. Additionally, independent clinical experts confirmed the generated policies are safe and accurately reflect user preferences.

</details>


### [161] [Efficient Equivariant Transformer for Self-Driving Agent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01466)
*Scott Xu,Dian Chen,Kelvin Wong,Chris Zhang,Kion Fallah,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveGATr：一种基于几何代数的SE(2)-等变Transformer架构，用于自动驾驶中的智能体建模，无需昂贵的成对相对位置编码，在计算成本与性能之间建立了优越的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中准确建模智能体行为是一个重要任务，该任务具有许多对称性（如智能体顺序的置换等变性、场景整体任意旋转平移的SE(2)-等变性）。现有方法使用成对相对位置编码实现SE(2)-等变性，但这种方法引入了与智能体数量平方相关的计算成本，限制了其在大规模场景和批处理中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出DriveGATr架构：1）将场景元素编码为2D投影几何代数ℝ*_{2,0,1}中的多向量；2）使用等变Transformer块堆栈处理这些多向量；3）关键创新：使用标准注意力机制在多向量之间建模几何关系，无需昂贵的显式成对相对位置编码。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的实验表明：1）DriveGATr在交通模拟任务上可与最先进方法相媲美；2）在性能与计算成本之间建立了优越的帕累托前沿；3）消除了二次计算成本，提高了大规模场景和批处理的可扩展性。

Conclusion: DriveGATr通过几何代数表示和标准注意力机制，实现了SE(2)-等变性而无需昂贵的成对相对位置编码，为自动驾驶智能体建模提供了一种计算高效且性能优越的解决方案，在计算成本与性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Accurately modeling agent behaviors is an important task in self-driving. It is also a task with many symmetries, such as equivariance to the order of agents and objects in the scene or equivariance to arbitrary roto-translations of the entire scene as a whole; i.e., SE(2)-equivariance. The transformer architecture is a ubiquitous tool for modeling these symmetries. While standard self-attention is inherently permutation equivariant, explicit pairwise relative positional encodings have been the standard for introducing SE(2)-equivariance. However, this approach introduces an additional cost that is quadratic in the number of agents, limiting its scalability to larger scenes and batch sizes. In this work, we propose DriveGATr, a novel transformer-based architecture for agent modeling that achieves SE(2)-equivariance without the computational cost of existing methods. Inspired by recent advances in geometric deep learning, DriveGATr encodes scene elements as multivectors in the 2D projective geometric algebra $\mathbb{R}^*_{2,0,1}$ and processes them with a stack of equivariant transformer blocks. Crucially, DriveGATr models geometric relationships using standard attention between multivectors, eliminating the need for costly explicit pairwise relative positional encodings. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that DriveGATr is comparable to the state-of-the-art in traffic simulation and establishes a superior Pareto front for performance vs computational cost.

</details>


### [162] [Distal-Stable Beam for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01490)
*Ryouichi Saito,Takahiro Koide,Yuya Tanaka,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型结构单元"远端稳定梁"，通过纯几何设计实现强刚度梯度，在保持中间段柔顺性的同时确保远端高刚性，为连续体机器人提供同时具备安全性和精度的新设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人适用于受限环境，但存在远端刚度低的问题，导致在外部载荷下产生较大的姿态误差。需要一种既能保持柔顺性又能确保远端稳定性的解决方案。

Method: 提出远端稳定梁结构单元，由两根平行杆和一根收敛杆通过导向盘约束构成。通过几何耦合抑制变形模式，保持远端姿态，实现纯几何设计的强刚度梯度。

Result: 实验表明远端刚度比中心高12倍，相比传统悬臂梁有约100倍的改进。该机制无需主动刚度调节即可同时实现柔顺性和远端稳定性。

Conclusion: 远端稳定梁为需要同时具备安全性和精度的连续体机器人提供了新的设计方法，通过纯几何设计实现了强刚度梯度，解决了传统连续体机器人远端刚度不足的问题。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for constrained environments but suffer from low distal stiffness, resulting in large posture errors under external loads. In this paper, we propose a novel structural primitive, the Distal-Stable Beam, which achieves a strong stiffness gradient through purely geometric design, maintaining compliance in the intermediate section while ensuring high distal rigidity. The structure consists of two parallel rods and one convergent rod constrained by guide disks, introducing geometric coupling that suppresses deformation modes and preserves distal posture. Experiments show that the distal stiffness is 12 times higher than at the center, corresponding to an approximately 100-fold improvement over a conventional cantilever beam. The proposed mechanism enables simultaneous compliance and distal stability without active stiffness modulation, providing a new design approach for continuum robots requiring both safety and precision.

</details>


### [163] [AnchorVLA: Anchored Diffusion for Efficient End-to-End Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01567)
*Jia Syuen Lim,Zhizhen Zhang,Peter Bohm,Brendan Tidd,Zi Huang,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnchorVLA：一种基于扩散的视觉语言动作策略，通过锚定扩散和轻量级自校正机制，在保持多模态动作生成的同时降低推理成本，提高移动操作任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作中的核心挑战是在保持多个合理动作模型的同时保持执行时的反应性。扩散策略虽然能建模多模态动作分布，但完整的迭代去噪在控制时成本高昂。动作分块虽然能分摊推理成本，但会导致部分开环行为，使小误差累积成漂移。

Method: AnchorVLA结合轻量级VLA适配骨干和锚定扩散动作头，后者在锚定轨迹周围进行局部去噪，使用截断扩散计划。为缓解分块引起的漂移，引入测试时自校正机制，通过轻量级残差校正模块在部署时进行高频、每步调整。

Result: 在多样化的移动操作任务中，AnchorVLA在干扰和分布偏移下提高了成功率和稳定性，同时保持了低延迟推理。

Conclusion: AnchorVLA通过锚定扩散和自校正机制，在保持多模态动作生成能力的同时显著降低了推理成本，解决了移动操作中反应性与动作多样性之间的平衡问题。

Abstract: A central challenge in mobile manipulation is preserving multiple plausible action models while remaining reactive during execution. A bottle in a cluttered scene can often be approached and grasped in multiple valid ways. Robust behavior depends on preserving this action diversity while remaining reactive as the scene evolves. Diffusion policies are appealing because they model multimodal action distributions rather than collapsing to one solution. But in practice, full iterative denoising is costly at control time. Action chunking helps amortize inference, yet it also creates partially open-loop behavior, allowing small mismatches to accumulate into drift. We present AnchorVLA, a diffusion-based VLA policy for mobile manipulation built on the core insight that when sampling begins near a plausible solution manifold, extensive denoising is unnecessary to recover multimodal, valid actions. AnchorVLA combines a lightweight VLA adaptation backbone with an anchored diffusion action head, which denoises locally around anchor trajectories using a truncated diffusion schedule. This retains multimodal action generation while reducing inference cost for closed-loop control. Crucially, to mitigate chunking-induced drift, we introduce a test-time self-correction mechanism via a lightweight residual correction module that makes high-frequency, per-step adjustments during rollout. Across diverse mobile manipulation tasks, AnchorVLA improves success and stability under disturbances and distribution shifts while maintaining low-latency inference. The source code is made available at https://github.com/jason-lim26/AnchorVLA.

</details>


### [164] [Boosting Vision-Language-Action Finetuning with Feasible Action Neighborhood Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01570)
*Haochen Niu,Kanyu Zhang,Shuyu Yin,Qinghai Guo,Peilin Liu,Fei Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAN引导的正则化方法，利用机器人操作中存在的可行动作邻域特性，通过高斯先验使模型输出分布与FAN几何对齐，提升VLA的样本效率和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人操作中，每个状态通常存在一个可行动作邻域（FAN）而非单一正确动作，但当前VLA训练方法直接从语言设置继承，未利用FAN特性，导致泛化能力差和样本效率低

Method: 引入FAN引导的正则化器，通过高斯先验使模型输出分布与FAN几何对齐，促进在首选方向和幅度周围的局部平滑和单峰预测

Result: 在强化微调（RFT）和监督微调（SFT）的广泛实验中，该方法在样本效率和成功率方面取得显著提升，在分布内和分布外场景均表现优异

Conclusion: 通过与物理操作内在动作容差对齐，FAN引导的正则化为样本高效且可泛化的VLA适应提供了原则性和实用的方法

Abstract: In real-world robotic manipulation, states typically admit a neighborhood of near-equivalent actions. That is, for each state, there exist a feasible action neighborhood (FAN) rather than a single correct action, within which motions yield indistinguishable progress. However, prevalent VLA training methodologies are directly inherited from linguistic settings and do not exploit the FAN property, thus leading to poor generalization and low sample efficiency. To address this limitation, we introduce a FAN-guided regularizer that shapes the model's output distribution to align with the geometry of FAN. Concretely, we introduce a Gaussian prior that promotes locally smooth and unimodal predictions around the preferred direction and magnitude. In extensive experiments across both reinforced finetuning (RFT) and supervised finetuning (SFT), our method achieves significant improvement in sample efficiency, and success rate in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. By aligning with the intrinsic action tolerance of physical manipulation, FAN-guided regularization provides a principled and practical method for sample-efficient, and generalizable VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [165] [Smooth Feedback Motion Planning with Reduced Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01614)
*Aref Amiri,Steven M. LaValle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在单纯形分解上计算高效的反馈运动规划方法，通过系统对齐局部向量场和构建最大星形链来生成更直接的轨迹，减少路径弯曲和控制能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单元分解的反馈运动规划算法常产生不必要的弯曲路径，导致运动速度慢、控制能耗高，需要更直接高效的轨迹生成方法。

Method: 1) 引入启发式方法系统对齐和分配局部向量场以产生更直接的轨迹；2) 提出新颖的几何算法构建围绕目标的最大星形单纯形链，形成大"漏斗"区域；3) 在该区域内应用最优直接到目标的控制律。

Result: 仿真显示：总弯曲减少平均91.40%，LQR控制能耗降低平均45.47%；与基于采样和优化的规划器比较证实了时间效率和鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算效率、路径直接性和控制能耗方面显著优于现有方法，适用于低维配置空间（d≤3），其中单纯形分解计算可行。

Abstract: Feedback motion planning over cell decompositions provides a robust method for generating collision-free robot motion with formal guarantees. However, existing algorithms often produce paths with unnecessary bending, leading to slower motion and higher control effort. This paper presents a computationally efficient method to mitigate this issue for a given simplicial decomposition. A heuristic is introduced that systematically aligns and assigns local vector fields to produce more direct trajectories, complemented by a novel geometric algorithm that constructs a maximal star-shaped chain of simplexes around the goal. This creates a large ``funnel'' in which an optimal, direct-to-goal control law can be safely applied. Simulations demonstrate that our method generates measurably more direct paths, reducing total bending by an average of 91.40\% and LQR control effort by an average of 45.47\%. Furthermore, comparative analysis against sampling-based and optimization-based planners confirms the time efficacy and robustness of our approach. While the proposed algorithms work over any finite-dimensional simplicial complex embedded in the collision-free subset of the configuration space, the practical application focuses on low-dimensional ($d\le3$) configuration spaces, where simplicial decomposition is computationally tractable.

</details>


### [166] [AURA: Multimodal Shared Autonomy for Real-World Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01659)
*Yukai Ma,Honglin He,Selina Song,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AURA是一个辅助城市机器人自主导航的多模态框架，将高层人类指令与低层AI控制解耦，通过空间感知指令编码器对齐视觉空间上下文，显著减少接管频率并提升导航稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂城市环境中的长时程导航依赖持续人工操作，导致疲劳、效率降低和安全问题。现有共享自治方法要求人类和AI在同一动作空间操作，造成高认知负荷。

Method: 提出AURA多模态框架，将城市导航分解为高层人类指令和低层AI控制。包含空间感知指令编码器对齐各种人类指令与视觉空间上下文。构建大规模数据集MM-CoS用于训练。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验显示，AURA能有效遵循人类指令，减少手动操作工作量，提高导航稳定性，支持在线适应。在类似接管条件下，共享自治框架将接管频率降低超过44%。

Conclusion: AURA通过解耦高层指令与低层控制，为复杂城市环境中的机器人导航提供了有效的共享自治解决方案，显著减轻人类操作负担并提升系统性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon navigation in complex urban environments relies heavily on continuous human operation, which leads to fatigue, reduced efficiency, and safety concerns. Shared autonomy, where a Vision-Language AI agent and a human operator collaborate on maneuvering the mobile machine, presents a promising solution to address these issues. However, existing shared autonomy methods often require humans and AI to operate within the same action space, leading to high cognitive overhead. We present Assistive Urban Robot Autonomy (AURA), a new multi-modal framework that decomposes urban navigation into high-level human instruction and low-level AI control. AURA incorporates a Spatial-Aware Instruction Encoder to align various human instructions with visual and spatial context. To facilitate training, we construct MM-CoS, a large-scale dataset comprising teleoperation and vision-language descriptions. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that AURA effectively follows human instructions, reduces manual operation effort, and improves navigation stability, while enabling online adaptation. Moreover, under similar takeover conditions, our shared autonomy framework reduces the frequency of takeovers by more than 44%. Demo video and more detail are provided in the project page.

</details>


### [167] [Bridging Large-Model Reasoning and Real-Time Control via Agentic Fast-Slow Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01681)
*Jiayi Chen,Shuai Wang,Guangxu Zhu,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Fast-Slow Planning分层框架，通过感知-决策-轨迹生成-控制解耦，结合VLM和LLM实现语义到可靠实时控制的映射，在CARLA中提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：要么让LLM直接生成轨迹（脆弱、难验证、延迟高），要么在线调整MPC目标（混合慢速决策与快速控制、接口模糊）。需要将语义意图映射到可靠实时控制的有效方法。

Method: 提出分层框架：1) Perception2Decision：车载VLM检测器压缩场景为以自我为中心的拓扑结构，云端LLM决策器映射为符号化驾驶指令；2) Decision2Trajectory：语义引导A*将语言指令嵌入软成本进行经典搜索，智能体精炼模块基于反馈和记忆调整规划器超参数；3) MPC实时跟踪轨迹，困难情况下可选云端引导参考。

Result: 在CARLA实验中，相比纯MPC和A*引导MPC基线，Agentic Fast-Slow Planning在扰动下提升鲁棒性，横向偏差减少达45%，完成时间减少超过12%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过解耦感知、推理、规划和控制到自然时间尺度，结合经典搜索与学习组件，实现了语义意图到可靠实时控制的映射，提高了自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Large foundation models enable powerful reasoning for autonomous systems, but mapping semantic intent to reliable real-time control remains challenging. Existing approaches either (i) let Large Language Models (LLMs) generate trajectories directly - brittle, hard to verify, and latency-prone - or (ii) adjust Model Predictive Control (MPC) objectives online - mixing slow deliberation with fast control and blurring interfaces. We propose Agentic Fast-Slow Planning, a hierarchical framework that decouples perception, reasoning, planning, and control across natural timescales. The framework contains two bridges. Perception2Decision compresses scenes into ego-centric topologies using an on-vehicle Vision-Language Model (VLM) detector, then maps them to symbolic driving directives in the cloud with an LLM decision maker - reducing bandwidth and delay while preserving interpretability. Decision2Trajectory converts directives into executable paths: Semantic-Guided A* embeds language-derived soft costs into classical search to bias solutions toward feasible trajectories, while an Agentic Refinement Module adapts planner hyperparameters using feedback and memory. Finally, MPC tracks the trajectories in real time, with optional cloud-guided references for difficult cases. Experiments in CARLA show that Agentic Fast-Slow Planning improves robustness under perturbations, reducing lateral deviation by up to 45% and completion time by over 12% compared to pure MPC and an A*-guided MPC baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/cjychenjiayi/icra2026_AFSP.

</details>


### [168] [3-D Relative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Angle and Self-Displacement Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01703)
*Chenyang Liang,Liangming Chen,Baoyi Cui,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于机器人间内角与自位移测量的三维相对定位框架，包含线性定位理论与抗噪MAP估计器，通过WTLS优化、神经密度估计和边缘化机制解决非凸性、先验未知和计算复杂度问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在测量噪声的情况下，利用机器人间局部测量实现相对定位是一个具有挑战性的问题，需要系统性的解决方案

Method: 1) 提出分布式线性相对定位算法及可定位性充分条件；2) 将线性计算过程重构为流形上的加权总体最小二乘优化问题；3) 结合神经密度估计器处理先验概率密度未知问题；4) 设计边缘化机制保持计算成本恒定

Result: 构建了系统性的三维相对定位框架，通过WTLS提供更精确的初始值降低局部最优风险，结合NDE处理先验未知问题，边缘化机制确保计算复杂度不随机器人移动而增加

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效处理测量噪声下的相对定位问题，通过线性理论、WTLS优化、神经密度估计和边缘化机制的系统性结合，实现了鲁棒且计算效率高的三维相对定位解决方案

Abstract: Realizing relative localization by leveraging inter-robot local measurements is a challenging problem, especially in the presence of measurement noise. Motivated by this challenge, in this paper we propose a novel and systematic 3-D relative localization framework based on inter-robot interior angle and self-displacement measurements. Initially, we propose a linear relative localization theory comprising a distributed linear relative localization algorithm and sufficient conditions for localizability. According to this theory, robots can determine their neighbors' relative positions and orientations in a purely linear manner. Subsequently, in order to deal with measurement noise, we present an advanced Maximum a Posterior (MAP) estimator by addressing three primary challenges existing in the MAP estimator. Firstly, it is common to formulate the MAP problem as an optimization problem, whose inherent non-convexity can result in local optima. To address this issue, we reformulate the linear computation process of the linear relative localization algorithm as a Weighted Total Least Squares (WTLS) optimization problem on manifolds. The optimal solution of the WTLS problem is more accurate, which can then be used as initial values when solving the optimization problem associated with the MAP problem, thereby reducing the risk of falling into local optima. The second challenge is the lack of knowledge of the prior probability density of the robots' relative positions and orientations at the initial time, which is required as an input for the MAP estimator. To deal with it, we combine the WTLS with a Neural Density Estimator (NDE). Thirdly, to prevent the increasing size of the relative positions and orientations to be estimated as the robots continuously move when solving the MAP problem, a marginalization mechanism is designed, which ensures that the computational cost remains constant.

</details>


### [169] [Hi-LOAM: Hierarchical Implicit Neural Fields for LiDAR Odometry and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01720)
*Zhiliu Yang,Jianyuan Zhang,Lianhui Zhao,Jinyu Dai,Zhu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi-LOAM：基于多尺度隐式神经表示的LiDAR里程计与建图框架，通过哈希表存储分层特征，采用无对应点扫描到隐式匹配进行位姿估计，实现自监督学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LOAM框架要么依赖监督信号，要么缺乏重建保真度，难以描绘大规模复杂场景的细节。需要克服这些限制，实现更精确的自监督定位与建图

Method: 1. 输入LiDAR点云，基于八叉树结构在多个层级的哈希表中学习和存储分层潜在特征；2. 通过浅层MLP将多尺度潜在特征解码为有符号距离值进行建图；3. 采用无对应点的扫描到隐式匹配范式估计最优位姿并将当前扫描注册到子地图中；4. 整个训练过程自监督进行，无需模型预训练

Result: 在多个真实世界和合成数据集上的广泛实验表明，Hi-LOAM在有效性和泛化能力方面优于现有最先进方法，展现出卓越性能

Conclusion: Hi-LOAM通过多尺度隐式神经表示和自监督学习，实现了高保真度的LiDAR里程计与建图，能够准确描绘大规模复杂场景的细节，具有良好的泛化能力

Abstract: LiDAR Odometry and Mapping (LOAM) is a pivotal technique for embodied-AI applications such as autonomous driving and robot navigation. Most existing LOAM frameworks are either contingent on the supervision signal, or lack of the reconstruction fidelity, which are deficient in depicting details of large-scale complex scenes. To overcome these limitations, we propose a multi-scale implicit neural localization and mapping framework using LiDAR sensor, called Hi-LOAM. Hi-LOAM receives LiDAR point cloud as the input data modality, learns and stores hierarchical latent features in multiple levels of hash tables based on an octree structure, then these multi-scale latent features are decoded into signed distance value through shallow Multilayer Perceptrons (MLPs) in the mapping procedure. For pose estimation procedure, we rely on a correspondence-free, scan-to-implicit matching paradigm to estimate optimal pose and register current scan into the submap. The entire training process is conducted in a self-supervised manner, which waives the model pre-training and manifests its generalizability when applied to diverse environments. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superior performance, in terms of the effectiveness and generalization capabilities, of our Hi-LOAM compared to existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [170] [Causal Scene Narration with Runtime Safety Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01723)
*Yun Li,Yidu Zhang,Simon Thompson,Ehsan Javanmardi,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CSN通过因果场景叙述重构VLA文本输入，结合安全监督和偏好对齐，显著提升自动驾驶性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶VLA模型处理导航指令、危险警告和交通状态描述等多样化文本输入时，这些信息通常以不连贯的片段形式呈现，迫使模型自行发现哪些环境约束与当前操作相关，这降低了系统的效率和可靠性。

Method: 提出因果场景叙述（CSN）方法，在推理时通过意图-约束对齐、定量基础和结构化分离来重构VLA文本输入，零GPU成本。同时结合基于单纯形法的运行时安全监督，以及使用带负对数似然正则化的Plackett-Luce DPO进行训练时对齐。

Result: 多城镇闭环CARLA评估显示，CSN将原始LMDrive的驾驶评分提升+31.1%，偏好对齐变体提升+24.5%。控制消融实验表明，因果结构贡献了39.1%的性能增益，其余归因于信息内容本身。感知噪声消融证实CSN的益处对现实传感误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义安全监督改善了违规评分，而反应式碰撞时间监控会降低性能，这表明VLA系统需要意图感知的监控。CSN通过结构化文本输入显著提升了自动驾驶VLA模型的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving must integrate diverse textual inputs, including navigation commands, hazard warnings, and traffic state descriptions, yet current systems often present these as disconnected fragments, forcing the model to discover on its own which environmental constraints are relevant to the current maneuver. We introduce Causal Scene Narration (CSN), which restructures VLA text inputs through intent-constraint alignment, quantitative grounding, and structured separation, at inference time with zero GPU cost. We complement CSN with Simplex-based runtime safety supervision and training-time alignment via Plackett-Luce DPO with negative log-likelihood (NLL) regularization. A multi-town closed-loop CARLA evaluation shows that CSN improves Driving Score by +31.1% on original LMDrive and +24.5% on the preference-aligned variant. A controlled ablation reveals that causal structure accounts for 39.1% of this gain, with the remainder attributable to information content alone. A perception noise ablation confirms that CSN's benefit is robust to realistic sensing errors. Semantic safety supervision improves Infraction Score, while reactive Time-To-Collision monitoring degrades performance, demonstrating that intent-aware monitoring is needed for VLA systems.

</details>


### [171] [Analysis of Efficient Transmission Methods of Grid Maps for Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01753)
*Robin Dehler,Dominik Authaler,Aryan Thakur,Thomas Wodtko,Michael Buchholz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于补丁的通信管道，利用现有压缩算法高效传输网格地图数据，解决了网格地图在分布式处理和V2X应用中数据量过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格地图是智能车辆环境建模的基础方法，但细胞级别的表示导致数据量过大。虽然补丁网格地图通过调整细胞大小在一定程度上缓解了这个问题，但其数据量仍然过大，无法满足新型分布式处理设置和V2X应用的需求。需要从通信角度解决网格地图数据传输的效率问题。

Method: 基于补丁网格地图方法，提出了一种补丁式通信管道，利用现有压缩算法高效传输网格地图数据。对车辆内部通信和V2X通信进行了全面分析，并通过两个真实世界实验设置验证了这些用例。

Result: 提出的通信管道能够有效压缩和传输网格地图数据，适用于车辆内部通信和V2X应用。通过真实实验验证了该方法的有效性，并为智能交通系统中网格地图数据的高效传输提供了推荐指南。

Conclusion: 补丁式通信管道结合现有压缩算法能够有效解决网格地图数据传输的效率问题，为智能交通系统中的分布式处理和V2X应用提供了可行的解决方案。论文总结了网格地图数据高效传输的推荐指南。

Abstract: Grid mapping is a fundamental approach to modeling the environment of intelligent vehicles or robots. Compared with object-based environment modeling, grid maps offer the distinct advantage of representing the environment without requiring any assumptions about objects, such as type or shape. For grid-map-based approaches, the environment is divided into cells, each containing information about its respective area, such as occupancy. This representation of the entire environment is crucial for achieving higher levels of autonomy. However, it has the drawback that modeling the scene at the cell level results in inherently large data sizes. Patched grid maps tackle this issue to a certain extent by adapting cell sizes in specific areas. Nevertheless, the data sizes of patched grid maps are still too large for novel distributed processing setups or vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. Our work builds on a patch-based grid-map approach and investigates the size problem from a communication perspective. To address this, we propose a patch-based communication pipeline that leverages existing compression algorithms to transmit grid-map data efficiently. We provide a comprehensive analysis of this pipeline for both intra-vehicle and V2X-based communication. The analysis is verified for these use cases with two real-world experiment setups. Finally, we summarize recommended guidelines for the efficient transmission of grid-map data in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [172] [Realistic Lip Motion Generation Based on 3D Dynamic Viseme and Coarticulation Modeling for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01756)
*Sheng Li,Jingcheng Huang,Min Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于3D动态视位和协同发音建模的唇部运动生成框架，用于人形机器人的逼真唇部同步


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逼真的唇部同步对于人形机器人自然的人机非语言交互至关重要，需要解决连续语音流中的运动冲突问题

Method: 基于中文发音理论构建3D动态视位库，采用声母-韵母解耦和能量调制的协同发音机制，将高维空间唇部运动重定向到14自由度唇部驱动系统

Result: 通过皮尔逊相关系数和平均绝对急动度指标进行定量消融实验，验证了所提架构的效率和准确性

Conclusion: 该研究为人形机器人的语音驱动唇部运动生成提供了一个轻量级、高效且高度实用的范式

Abstract: Realistic lip synchronization is essential for the natural human-robot non-verbal interaction of humanoid robots. Motivated by this need, this paper presents a lip motion generation framework based on 3D dynamic viseme and coarticulation modeling. By analyzing Chinese pronunciation theory, a 3D dynamic viseme library is constructed based on the ARKit standard, which offers coherent prior trajectories of lips. To resolve motion conflicts within continuous speech streams, a coarticulation mechanism is developed by incorporating initial-final (Shengmu-Yunmu) decoupling and energy modulation. After developing a strategy to retarget high-dimensional spatial lip motion to a 14-DOF lip actuation system of a humanoid head platform, the efficiency and accuracy of the proposed architecture is experimentally validated and demonstrated with quantitative ablation experiments using the metrics of the Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) and the Mean Absolute Jerk (MAJ). This research offers a lightweight, efficient, and highly practical paradigm for the speech-driven lip motion generation of humanoid robots. The 3D dynamic viseme library and real-world deployment videos are available at {https://github.com/yuesheng21/Phoneme-to-Lip-14DOF}

</details>


### [173] [Preferential Bayesian Optimization with Crash Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01776)
*Johanna Menn,David Stenger,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrashPBO：一种在偏好贝叶斯优化中整合实验崩溃反馈的机制，减少硬件系统优化过程中的崩溃次数并提高数据效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人硬件系统（如四旋翼控制）的参数优化中，标准偏好贝叶斯优化无法处理实验崩溃的情况。崩溃会导致耗时的实验重置、设备磨损等不良后果，但现有方法无法利用崩溃反馈，导致频繁探索会导致崩溃的参数。

Method: 提出CrashPBO机制，允许用户在优化过程中同时表达偏好（通过成对比较）和报告实验崩溃。该方法扩展了标准偏好贝叶斯优化框架，将崩溃信息整合到优化过程中，避免探索已知会导致崩溃的参数区域。

Result: 在合成函数上的基准测试显示，CrashPBO机制将崩溃次数减少了63%，并提高了数据效率。在三个机器人平台上的实验证明了该方法的广泛适用性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: CrashPBO为基于人类反馈（偏好和崩溃）的参数学习提供了一个灵活、用户友好的框架，特别适用于硬件系统的优化，能够显著减少实验崩溃并提高优化效率。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a popular black-box optimization method for parameter learning in control and robotics. It typically requires an objective function that reflects the user's optimization goal. However, in practical applications, this objective function is often inaccessible due to complex or unmeasurable performance metrics. Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) overcomes this limitation by leveraging human feedback through pairwise comparisons, eliminating the need for explicit performance quantification. When applying PBO to hardware systems, such as in quadcopter control, crashes can cause time-consuming experimental resets, wear and tear, or otherwise undesired outcomes. Standard PBO methods cannot incorporate feedback from such crashed experiments, resulting in the exploration of parameters that frequently lead to experimental crashes. We thus introduce CrashPBO, a user-friendly mechanism that enables users to both express preferences and report crashes during the optimization process. Benchmarking on synthetic functions shows that this mechanism reduces crashes by 63% and increases data efficiency. Through experiments on three robotics platforms, we demonstrate the wide applicability and transferability of CrashPBO, highlighting that it provides a flexible, user-friendly framework for parameter learning with human feedback on preferences and crashes.

</details>


### [174] [Posterior Optimization with Clipped Objective for Bridging Efficiency and Stability in Generative Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01860)
*Yuhui Chen,Haoran Li,Zhennan Jiang,Yuxing Qin,Yuxuan Wan,Weiheng Liu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POCO是一个用于机器人操作的强化学习框架，通过将策略改进形式化为后验推断问题，采用期望最大化过程将奖励加权的隐式后验蒸馏到策略中，无需似然估计，防止灾难性策略崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管表达能力强的生成模型能够捕捉复杂多模态的动作分布，但通过强化学习微调这些策略仍然面临不稳定和样本效率低的问题。需要一种能够稳定高效微调预训练策略的方法。

Method: POCO将策略改进形式化为后验推断问题，采用期望最大化过程将奖励加权的隐式后验蒸馏到策略中，无需似然估计。采用离线到在线范式，将在线探索锚定到预训练先验，模型无关设计可微调大型VLA模型而无需架构修改。

Result: 在7个仿真基准和4个接触丰富的真实世界任务中，POCO防止了灾难性策略崩溃，优于最先进的基线方法，在真实世界任务中实现了96.7%的成功率。

Conclusion: POCO提供了一个原则性的强化学习框架，能够稳定高效地微调表达能力强的生成模型，在仿真和真实世界任务中都表现出色，为机器人操作策略的改进提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Expressive generative models have advanced robotic manipulation by capturing complex, multi-modal action distributions over temporally extended trajectories. However, fine-tuning these policies via RL remains challenging due to instability and sample inefficiency. We introduce Posterior Optimization with Clipped Objective (POCO), a principled RL framework that formulates policy improvement as a posterior inference problem tailored for temporal action chunks. Through an Expectation-Maximization procedure, POCO distills a reward-weighted implicit posterior into the policy without likelihood estimation. Furthermore, POCO adopts an offline-to-online paradigm that anchors online exploration to pre-trained priors, and its model-agnostic design scales to fine-tune large VLA models without architectural modifications. Evaluations across 7 simulation benchmarks and 4 contact-rich real-world tasks demonstrate that POCO prevents catastrophic policy collapse, outperforms SOTA baselines, and achieves a 96.7% success rate on real-world tasks. Videos are available at our project website https://cccedric.github.io/poco/.

</details>


### [175] [Integrated Identification of Collaborative Robots for Robot Assisted 3D Printing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01991)
*Alessandro Dimauro,Davide Tebaldi,Fabio Pini,Luigi Biagiotti,Francesco Leali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的集成参数识别方法，用于提升机器人辅助增材制造系统的动态建模精度，通过五步识别流程确保参数物理一致性，并在6自由度协作机器人上验证了模型有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助增材制造虽然克服了传统笛卡尔系统的尺寸和运动学限制，但机器人动态复杂性增加带来了精度、控制和误差预测方面的挑战。协作机器人通常存在传感器和编程限制，需要可靠的动态模型来提升系统性能。

Method: 提出集成参数识别方法，包含五个步骤：1)几何和惯性分析；2)摩擦参数识别；3)控制器参数识别；4)剩余参数识别。该方法确保识别参数的物理一致性，适用于机器人、执行器和控制器的整体系统建模。

Result: 在6自由度协作机器人的热塑性挤出工艺案例研究中验证了识别方法。实验结果显示，识别模型与实际机器人的实验结果匹配度非常好，证明了该方法在提升机器人辅助3D打印过程中的精度、控制和误差预测方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 集成参数识别方法能够获得可靠的动态模型，即使在协作机器人典型的传感器和编程限制下也能有效工作。该方法为机器人辅助增材制造系统的精度提升和控制优化提供了有效的建模基础。

Abstract: In recent years, the integration of additive manufacturing (AM) and industrial robotics has opened new perspectives for the production of complex components, particularly in the automotive sector. Robot-assisted additive manufacturing processes overcome the dimensional and kinematic limitations of traditional Cartesian systems, enabling non-planar deposition and greater geometric flexibility. However, the increasing dynamic complexity of robotic manipulators introduces challenges related to precision, control, and error prediction. This work proposes a model-based approach equipped with an integrated identification procedure of the system's parameters, including the robot, the actuators and the controllers. We show that the integrated modeling procedure allows to obtain a reliable dynamic model even in the presence of sensory and programming limitations typical of collaborative robots. The manipulator's dynamic model is identified through an integrated five step methodology: starting with geometric and inertial analysis, followed by friction and controller parameters identification, all the way to the remaining parameters identification. The proposed procedure intrinsically ensures the physical consistency of the identified parameters. The identification approach is validated on a real world case study involving a 6-Degrees-Of-Freedom (DoFs) collaborative robot used in a thermoplastic extrusion process. The very good matching between the experimental results given by actual robot and those given by the identified model shows the potential enhancement of precision, control, and error prediction in Robot Assisted 3D Printing Processes.

</details>


### [176] [Bridging Discrete Planning and Continuous Execution for Redundant Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02021)
*Teng Yan,Yue Yu,Yihan Liu,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种连接离散规划与连续执行的桥梁框架，通过改进规划侧的动作设计和执行侧的逆运动学层，显著提升了7自由度冗余机械臂在体素网格强化学习路径规划中的执行质量和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素网格强化学习在冗余机械臂路径规划中应用广泛，但直接通过逐点数值逆运动学在7自由度机械臂上执行时，会出现步长抖动、关节突变和奇异点附近不稳定等问题，需要改进离散规划与连续执行之间的衔接。

Method: 提出桥梁框架：规划侧引入步长归一化的26邻域笛卡尔动作和几何平局打破机制来抑制不必要转向和消除步长振荡；执行侧实现任务优先级阻尼最小二乘逆运动学层，将末端执行器位置作为主要任务，姿态和关节居中作为次要任务在零空间处理，结合信任区域裁剪和关节速度约束。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂的随机稀疏、中等和密集环境中，该桥梁框架将密集场景的规划成功率从约0.58提升到1.00，代表性路径长度从约1.53米缩短到1.10米，在保持末端执行器误差低于1毫米的同时，将峰值关节加速度降低了一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该桥梁框架显著提高了体素网格强化学习路径在冗余机械臂上的连续执行质量，通过改进规划动作设计和执行逆运动学，有效解决了离散规划与连续执行之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Voxel-grid reinforcement learning is widely adopted for path planning in redundant manipulators due to its simplicity and reproducibility. However, direct execution through point-wise numerical inverse kinematics on 7-DoF arms often yields step-size jitter, abrupt joint transitions, and instability near singular configurations. This work proposes a bridging framework between discrete planning and continuous execution without modifying the discrete planner itself. On the planning side, step-normalized 26-neighbor Cartesian actions and a geometric tie-breaking mechanism are introduced to suppress unnecessary turns and eliminate step-size oscillations. On the execution side, a task-priority damped least-squares (TP-DLS) inverse kinematics layer is implemented. This layer treats end-effector position as a primary task, while posture and joint centering are handled as subordinate tasks projected into the null space, combined with trust-region clipping and joint velocity constraints. On a 7-DoF manipulator in random sparse, medium, and dense environments, this bridge raises planning success in dense scenes from about 0.58 to 1.00, shortens representative path length from roughly 1.53 m to 1.10 m, and while keeping end-effector error below 1 mm, reduces peak joint accelerations by over an order of magnitude, substantially improving the continuous execution quality of voxel-based RL paths on redundant manipulators.

</details>


### [177] [O-ConNet: Geometry-Aware End-to-End Inference of Over-Constrained Spatial Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02038)
*Haoyu Sun,Meng Zhao,Tianhao Wang,Jianxu Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: O-ConNet：一种端到端框架，仅需三个稀疏可达点即可推断空间过约束机构的结构参数并重建完整运动轨迹，无需在推理时显式求解约束方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在科学发现中展现出强大潜力，但其建模宏观刚体运动学约束的能力尚未充分探索。本研究旨在探索深度学习在空间过约束机构上的应用，解决从稀疏观测中推断机构参数和运动轨迹的问题。

Method: 提出O-ConNet端到端框架，仅使用三个稀疏可达点作为输入，通过深度学习直接推断机构的结构参数并重建完整运动轨迹。该方法避免了在推理过程中显式求解复杂的约束方程。

Result: 在自建的Bennett 4R数据集（42,860个有效样本）上，O-ConNet取得Param-MAE 0.276±0.077和Traj-MAE 0.145±0.018（10次运行均值±标准差），分别比最强序列基线（LSTM-Seq2Seq）提升65.1%和88.2%。

Conclusion: 端到端学习能够捕捉闭环几何结构，为在极端稀疏观测条件下的空间过约束机构逆向设计提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown strong potential for scientific discovery, but its ability to model macroscopic rigid-body kinematic constraints remains underexplored. We study this problem on spatial over-constrained mechanisms and propose O-ConNet, an end-to-end framework that infers mechanism structural parameters from only three sparse reachable points while reconstructing the full motion trajectory, without explicitly solving constraint equations during inference. On a self-constructed Bennett 4R dataset of 42,860 valid samples, O-ConNet achieves Param-MAE 0.276 +/- 0.077 and Traj-MAE 0.145 +/- 0.018 (mean +/- std over 10 runs), outperforming the strongest sequence baseline (LSTM-Seq2Seq) by 65.1 percent and 88.2 percent, respectively. These results suggest that end-to-end learning can capture closed-loop geometric structure and provide a practical route for inverse design of spatial over-constrained mechanisms under extremely sparse observations.

</details>


### [178] [HyVGGT-VO: Tightly Coupled Hybrid Dense Visual Odometry with Feed-Forward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02107)
*Junxiang Pan,Lipu Zhou,Baojie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyVGGT-VO提出了一种结合稀疏视觉里程计计算效率和前馈模型稠密重建能力的新型框架，通过自适应混合跟踪前端和分层优化实现了5倍速度提升和显著精度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密视觉里程计方法存在计算负担重、无法实时输出位姿的问题，而传统稀疏方法虽然计算效率高但缺乏稠密重建能力。需要一种既能保持计算效率又能实现稠密重建的解决方案。

Method: 提出HyVGGT-VO框架，将传统VO框架与VGGT前馈模型紧密耦合。设计自适应混合跟踪前端，在传统光流和VGGT跟踪头之间动态切换以确保鲁棒性；引入分层优化框架，联合优化VO位姿和VGGT预测尺度以保证全局尺度一致性。

Result: 相比现有VGGT方法实现约5倍处理速度提升；在室内EuRoC数据集上平均轨迹误差降低85%，在室外KITTI基准上降低12%。

Conclusion: HyVGGT-VO成功结合了稀疏VO的计算效率和前馈模型的稠密重建能力，首次实现了传统VO框架与VGGT的紧密耦合，在保持实时性的同时显著提升了精度。

Abstract: Dense visual odometry (VO), which provides pose estimation and dense 3D reconstruction, serves as the cornerstone for applications ranging from robotics to augmented reality. Recently, feed-forward models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in dense mapping. However, when these models are used in dense visual SLAM systems, their heavy computational burden restricts them to yielding sparse pose outputs at keyframes while still failing to achieve real-time pose estimation. In contrast, traditional sparse methods provide high computational efficiency and high-frequency pose outputs, but lack the capability for dense reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose HyVGGT-VO, a novel framework that combines the computational efficiency of sparse VO with the dense reconstruction capabilities of feed-forward models. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tightly couple a traditional VO framework with VGGT, a state-of-the-art feed-forward model. Specifically, we design an adaptive hybrid tracking frontend that dynamically switches between traditional optical flow and the VGGT tracking head to ensure robustness. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical optimization framework that jointly refines VO poses and the scale of VGGT predictions to ensure global scale consistency. Our approach achieves an approximately 5x processing speedup compared to existing VGGT-based methods, while reducing the average trajectory error by 85% on the indoor EuRoC dataset and 12% on the outdoor KITTI benchmark. Our code will be publicly available upon acceptance. Project page: https://geneta2580.github.io/HyVGGT-VO.io.

</details>


### [179] [Cross-Modal Visuo-Tactile Object Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02108)
*Anirvan Dutta,Simone Tasciotti,Claudia Cusseddu,Ang Li,Panayiota Poirazi,Julijana Gjorgjieva,Etienne Burdet,Patrick van der Smagt,Mohsen Kaboli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Modal Latent Filter (CMLF)框架，通过贝叶斯推理学习物理对象属性的结构化因果潜在状态空间，实现视觉与触觉的双向跨模态先验传递，提升机器人物理属性估计的效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人操作需要准确估计物理属性以确保安全高效，特别是在接触丰富的交互中。视觉和触觉提供互补信息，但物理属性只能间接观测且难以精确建模。现有方法主要关注强制传感器融合或静态跨模态对齐，缺乏对不确定性和信念随时间演化的考虑。

Method: 受人类多感官感知和主动推理启发，提出Cross-Modal Latent Filter (CMLF)框架。该方法学习物理对象属性的结构化因果潜在状态空间，支持视觉与触觉之间的双向跨模态先验传递，并通过随时间演化的贝叶斯推理过程整合感官证据。

Result: 真实世界机器人实验表明，CMLF在不确定性条件下比基线方法更高效、更鲁棒地估计潜在物理属性。模型还表现出类似人类的感知耦合现象，包括对跨模态错觉的敏感性和学习跨感官关联的相似轨迹。

Conclusion: CMLF是实现机器人多感官感知中可泛化、鲁棒且物理一致的跨模态集成的重要进展，为自主机器人操作中的物理属性估计提供了新框架。

Abstract: Estimating physical properties is critical for safe and efficient autonomous robotic manipulation, particularly during contact-rich interactions. In such settings, vision and tactile sensing provide complementary information about object geometry, pose, inertia, stiffness, and contact dynamics, such as stick-slip behavior. However, these properties are only indirectly observable and cannot always be modeled precisely (e.g., deformation in non-rigid objects coupled with nonlinear contact friction), making the estimation problem inherently complex and requiring sustained exploitation of visuo-tactile sensory information during action. Existing visuo-tactile perception frameworks have primarily emphasized forceful sensor fusion or static cross-modal alignment, with limited consideration of how uncertainty and beliefs about object properties evolve over time. Inspired by human multi-sensory perception and active inference, we propose the Cross-Modal Latent Filter (CMLF) to learn a structured, causal latent state-space of physical object properties. CMLF supports bidirectional transfer of cross-modal priors between vision and touch and integrates sensory evidence through a Bayesian inference process that evolves over time. Real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that CMLF improves the efficiency and robustness of latent physical properties estimation under uncertainty compared to baseline approaches. Beyond performance gains, the model exhibits perceptual coupling phenomena analogous to those observed in humans, including susceptibility to cross-modal illusions and similar trajectories in learning cross-sensory associations. Together, these results constitutes a significant step toward generalizable, robust and physically consistent cross-modal integration for robotic multi-sensory perception.

</details>


### [180] [ROS 2-Based LiDAR Perception Framework for Mobile Robots in Dynamic Production Environments, Utilizing Synthetic Data Generation, Transformation-Equivariant 3D Detection and Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02109)
*Lukas Bergs,Tan Chung,Marmik Thakkar,Alexander Moriz,Amon Göppert,Chinnawut Nantabut,Robert Schmitt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于ROS的LiDAR框架，结合合成数据训练的变换等变3D检测与基于中心位姿的多目标跟踪，提升工业移动机械臂的感知鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态生产环境中自适应机器人需要鲁棒的感知能力，包括6D位姿估计和多目标跟踪。现有方法存在对真实数据依赖、噪声鲁棒性不足以及时空一致性等问题

Method: 基于ROS的LiDAR框架，集成合成数据训练的变换等变3D检测方法，并采用基于中心位姿的多目标跟踪技术

Result: 在72个场景中验证，单独位姿估计IoU为62.6%，结合多目标跟踪后提升至83.12%。LiDAR框架实现91.12%的高阶跟踪准确率

Conclusion: 该LiDAR框架显著提升了工业移动机械臂感知系统的鲁棒性和通用性，为动态生产环境中的自适应机器人提供了有效的感知解决方案

Abstract: Adaptive robots in dynamic production environments require robust perception capabilities, including 6D pose estimation and multi-object tracking. To address limitations in real-world data dependency, noise robustness, and spatiotemporal consistency, a LiDAR framework based on the Robot Operating System integrating a synthetic-data-trained Transformation-Equivariant 3D Detection with multi-object-tracking leveraging center poses is proposed. Validated across 72 scenarios with motion capture technology, overall results yield an Intersection over Union of 62.6% for standalone pose estimation, rising to 83.12% with multi-object-tracking integration. Our LiDAR-based framework achieves 91.12% of Higher Order Tracking Accuracy, advancing robustness and versatility of LiDAR-based perception systems for industrial mobile manipulators.

</details>


### [181] [PRO-SPECT: Probabilistically Safe Scalable Planning for Energy-Aware Coordinated UAV-UGV Teams in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02142)
*Roger Fowler,Cahit Ikbal Er,Benjamin Johnsenberg,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRO-SPECT算法，为无人机-无人车团队在随机环境中进行风险约束的能源感知路径规划，确保能量耗尽概率不超过用户指定风险水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设确定性旅行时间或使用固定鲁棒性边界，无法有效处理随机环境中的不确定性。需要开发能够在整个任务中约束故障概率的风险感知规划方法。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数规划，提出PRO-SPECT多项式时间算法，支持离线规划和在线重规划，在扰动下保持风险约束。

Result: 提供解决方案可行性和时间复杂度的理论结果，通过数值比较和仿真验证方法性能。

Conclusion: PRO-SPECT算法能够为无人机-无人车团队生成风险约束的能源感知规划，在随机环境中有效管理不确定性，同时支持动态适应。

Abstract: We consider energy-aware planning for an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) team operating in a stochastic environment. The UAV must visit a set of air points in minimum time while respecting energy constraints, relying on the UGV as a mobile charging station. Unlike prior work that assumed deterministic travel times or used fixed robustness margins, we model travel times as random variables and bound the probability of failure (energy depletion) across the entire mission to a user-specified risk level. We formulate the problem as a Mixed-Integer Program and propose PRO-SPECT, a polynomial-time algorithm that generates risk-bounded plans. The algorithm supports both offline planning and online re-planning, enabling the team to adapt to disturbances while preserving the risk bound. We provide theoretical results on solution feasibility and time complexity. We also demonstrate the performance of our method via numerical comparisons and simulations.

</details>


### [182] [A virtual-variable-length method for robust inverse kinematics of multi-segment continuum robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02256)
*Weiting Feng,Federico Renda,Yunjie Yang,Francesco Giorgio-Serchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出虚拟变长(VVL)方法解决多段连续体机械臂逆运动学问题，相比传统雅可比方法提升20%收敛成功率，减少40-80%平均迭代次数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于雅可比的逆运动学求解器在从中性/静止配置初始化时，通常收敛缓慢，在某些条件下可能无法收敛（死锁）。需要一种更稳健的方法来解决多段连续体机械臂的逆运动学问题。

Method: 提出虚拟变长(VVL)方法，在求解迭代过程中引入段长度的虚拟变化，赋予虚拟轴向自由度，从而缓解不利行为和约束，实现或加速收敛。

Result: 通过超过180万次随机试验（涵盖2-7段机械臂），VVL方法相比基准雅可比和阻尼最小二乘求解器，收敛成功率提升高达20%，在相同精度阈值(10^-4-10^-8)下平均迭代次数减少40-80%。

Conclusion: VVL方法能有效解决多段连续体机械臂逆运动学求解中的收敛问题，特别是对边界邻近配置导致的死锁情况有显著改善，提高了求解的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new, robust method to solve the inverse kinematics (IK) of multi-segment continuum manipulators. Conventional Jacobian-based solvers, especially when initialized from neutral/rest configurations, often exhibit slow convergence and, in certain conditions, may fail to converge (deadlock). The Virtual-Variable-Length (VVL) method proposed here introduces fictitious variations of segments' length during the solution iteration, conferring virtual axial degrees of freedom that alleviate adverse behaviors and constraints, thus enabling or accelerating convergence. Comprehensive numerical experiments were conducted to compare the VVL method against benchmark Jacobian-based and Damped Least Square IK solvers. Across more than $1.8\times 10^6$ randomized trials covering manipulators with two to seven segments, the proposed approach achieved up to a 20$\%$ increase in convergence success rate over the benchmark and a 40-80$\%$ reduction in average iteration count under equivalent accuracy thresholds ($10^{-4}-10^{-8}$). While deadlocks are not restricted to workspace boundaries and may occur at arbitrary poses, our empirical study identifies boundary-proximal configurations as a frequent cause of failed convergence and the VVL method mitigates such occurrences over a statistical sample of test cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Runtime Burden Allocation for Structured LLM Routing in Agentic Expert Systems: A Full-Factorial Cross-Backend Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01235)
*Zhou Hanlin,Chan Huah Yong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将结构化LLM路由重新定义为系统级负载分配问题，而非单纯的提示工程问题，并通过跨后端实验发现路由性能高度依赖于特定后端，不存在通用的最佳路由模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型成为智能体AI系统的核心控制组件，可靠的结构化路由需要在正确性、延迟和实现成本之间取得平衡。传统上将其视为提示工程问题，但作者认为这本质上是系统级的负载分配问题，需要考虑结构工作在生成栈中的分配方式。

Method: 采用全面的全因子基准测试，涵盖48种部署配置和15,552个请求，覆盖OpenAI、Gemini和Llama三种后端。研究评估了不同结构分配策略：模型直接输出结构、传输中压缩结构、生成后本地重建结构等模式。

Result: 核心发现是：不存在通用的最佳路由模式，后端特定的交互效应主导性能。在Gemini和OpenAI上保持高可靠性的模式，在Llama上可能出现显著的正确性下降；压缩实现的效率增益也高度依赖于后端。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个可部署的框架，用于在异构后端条件下推理结构化路由问题。提供了跨后端评估方法和实际部署指导，帮助在生产级智能体专家系统中导航正确性-成本-延迟的权衡边界。

Abstract: Structured LLM routing is often treated as a prompt-engineering problem. We argue that it is, more fundamentally, a systems-level burden-allocation problem. As large language models (LLMs) become core control components in agentic AI systems, reliable structured routing must balance correctness, latency, and implementation cost under real deployment constraints. We show that this balance is shaped not only by prompts or schemas, but also by how structural work is allocated across the generation stack: whether output structure is emitted directly by the model, compressed during transport, or reconstructed locally after generation.
  We evaluate this formulation through a comprehensive full-factorial benchmark covering 48 deployment configurations and 15,552 requests across OpenAI, Gemini, and Llama backends. Our central finding is consequential: there is no universal best routing mode. Instead, backend-specific interaction effects dominate performance. Modes that remain highly reliable on Gemini and OpenAI can suffer substantial correctness degradation on Llama, while efficiency gains from compressed realization are strongly backend-dependent.
  Rather than presenting another isolated model comparison, this work contributes a deployable framework for reasoning about structured routing under heterogeneous backend conditions. We provide a cross-backend evaluation methodology and practical deployment guidance for navigating the correctness-cost-latency frontier in production-grade agentic expert systems.

</details>


### [184] [Crashing Waves vs. Rising Tides: Preliminary Findings on AI Automation from Thousands of Worker Evaluations of Labor Market Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01363)
*Matthias Mertens,Adam Kuzee,Brittany S. Harris,Harry Lyu,Wensu Li,Jonathan Rosenfeld,Meiri Anto,Martin Fleming,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出AI自动化存在两种模式：崩溃波（能力在少数任务上急剧提升）和涨潮（能力在广泛任务上持续提升）。基于对3000多个文本任务的评估，研究发现涨潮是主要形式，AI在2024年Q2能以50%成功率完成人类3-4小时的任务，预计到2029年能在大多数文本任务上达到80%-95%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解AI自动化的本质模式，区分能力提升是集中在少数任务上的"崩溃波"式突变，还是在广泛任务上持续增长的"涨潮"式演进。这有助于更准确地预测AI对经济和劳动力市场的影响。

Method: 基于美国劳工部O*NET分类，构建了3000多个文本型任务数据集，这些任务可由LLM处理。通过来自相关岗位的工人进行了超过17000次评估，测试AI在不同任务上的表现。

Result: 研究发现几乎没有崩溃波的证据，但存在显著的涨潮效应。AI在2024年Q2能以约50%成功率完成人类3-4小时的任务，预计到2025年Q3提升至65%。如果趋势持续，到2029年AI在大多数文本任务上的平均成功率将达到80%-95%。

Conclusion: AI自动化主要表现为涨潮模式，即能力在广泛任务上持续提升而非集中在少数任务上爆发。这种渐进但广泛的改进意味着AI将逐步影响更多工作岗位，但组织采用AI可能需要更长时间，从而缓冲对劳动力市场的冲击。

Abstract: We propose that AI automation is a continuum between: (i) crashing waves where AI capabilities surge abruptly over small sets of tasks, and (ii) rising tides where the increase in AI capabilities is more continuous and broad-based. We test for these effects in preliminary evidence from an ongoing evaluation of AI capabilities across over 3,000 broad-based tasks derived from the U.S. Department of Labor O*NET categorization that are text-based and thus LLM-addressable. Based on more than 17,000 evaluations by workers from these jobs, we find little evidence of crashing waves (in contrast to recent work by METR), but substantial evidence that rising tides are the primary form of AI automation. AI performance is high and improving rapidly across a wide range of tasks. We estimate that, in 2024-Q2, AI models successfully complete tasks that take humans approximately 3-4 hours with about a 50% success rate, increasing to about 65% by 2025-Q3. If recent trends in AI capability growth persist, this pace of AI improvement implies that LLMs will be able to complete most text-related tasks with success rates of, on average, 80%-95% by 2029 at a minimally sufficient quality level. Achieving near-perfect success rates at this quality level or comparable success rates at superior quality would require several additional years. These AI capability improvements would impact the economy and labor market as organizations adopt AI, which could have a substantially longer timeline.

</details>


### [185] [The Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework: A Validation Architecture for Simulated Potential Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01325)
*Olav Laudy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出数字孪生反事实框架(DTCF)，通过模拟而非统计估计反事实结果，建立分层验证体系将不可证伪的因果主张转化为可检验的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断方法都依赖于不可观测的假设(如可忽略性、平行趋势等)来填补缺失的反事实数据，但无法直接产生反事实结果本身。需要一种新框架来更直接地处理反事实问题。

Method: 提出数字孪生反事实框架：1) 使用数字孪生模拟器在潜在结果框架内进行随机映射；2) 建立孪生保真度假设层次(边际保真度、联合保真度、结构保真度)；3) 设计五级验证架构将不可证伪的主张转化为可检验的假设；4) 将因果量分解为可边际验证和依赖copula的部分；5) 提供边界、敏感性和不确定性量化工具。

Result: 该框架使边际因果主张(如ATE、CATE、QTE)可通过可观测数据检验，而联合因果主张(如ITE分布、获益/损害概率、处理效应方差)则明确依赖于不可观测的个体内依赖结构。框架不解决因果推断的根本问题，但提供了系统化处理反事实模拟和验证的方法。

Conclusion: DTCF为因果推断提供了一个新范式：边际因果主张变得可检验，联合因果主张变得明确假设索引化，两者之间的差距被形式化表征。框架将不可证伪的因果主张转化为可检验的假设，使因果推断更加透明和可验证。

Abstract: The fundamental problem of causal inference - that the counterfactual outcome for any individual is never observed - has shaped the entire methodology of the field. Every existing approach substitutes assumptions for missing data: ignorability, parallel trends, exclusion restrictions. None produces the counterfactual itself. This paper proposes the Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework (DTCF): rather than estimating the counterfactual statistically, we simulate it using a digital twin and subject the simulation to a hierarchical validation regime. We formalize the digital twin simulator as a stochastic mapping within the potential outcomes framework and introduce a hierarchy of twin fidelity assumptions - from marginal fidelity through joint fidelity to structural fidelity - each unlocking a progressively richer class of estimands. The central contribution is threefold. First, a five-level validation architecture converts the unfalsifiable claim that the simulator produces correct counterfactuals into falsifiable tests against observable data. Second, a formal decomposition separates causal quantities into those that are marginally validated (ATE, CATE, QTE - testable through observable-arm comparison) and those that are copula-dependent (the ITE distribution, probability of benefit/harm, variance of treatment effects - permanently reliant on the unobservable within-individual dependence structure). Third, bounding, sensitivity, and uncertainty quantification tools make the copula dependence explicit. The DTCF does not resolve the fundamental problem of causal inference. What it provides is a framework in which marginal causal claims become increasingly testable, joint causal claims become explicitly assumption-indexed, and the gap between the two is formally characterized.

</details>


### [186] [IDEA2: Expert-in-the-loop competency question elicitation for collaborative ontology engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01344)
*Elliott Watkiss-Leek,Reham Alharbi,Harry Rostron,Andrew Ng,Ewan Johnson,Andrew Mitchell,Terry R. Payne,Valentina Tamma,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IDEA2是一个集成大语言模型的半自动化工作流，用于解决本体工程中能力问题（CQ）提取的资源密集型瓶颈，通过专家在环的协作过程提高CQ的接受度和相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力问题提取是本体工程中关键但资源密集的瓶颈阶段，主要挑战在于领域专家（拥有知识）和本体工程师（形式化知识）之间的沟通鸿沟，需要更高效的协作方法。

Method: IDEA2采用半自动化工作流，核心是迭代循环：1）基于LLM从需求文档中提取初始CQ；2）领域专家在协作平台上进行共同创造的评审和反馈；3）LLM基于反馈迭代重新表述被拒绝的CQ直至达成共识。整个过程使用溯源模型跟踪CQ的完整生命周期，包括编辑谱系、匿名反馈和生成参数。

Result: 在2个真实场景（科学数据、文化遗产）中验证表明，IDEA2能够加速需求工程过程，提高最终CQ的接受度和相关性，并在领域专家中表现出高可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: IDEA2通过集成LLM和专家在环的协作过程，有效解决了本体工程中CQ提取的瓶颈问题，提供了透明、可复现的工作流，显著提高了需求工程的效率和质量。

Abstract: Competency question (CQ) elicitation represents a critical but resource-intensive bottleneck in ontology engineering. This foundational phase is often hampered by the communication gap between domain experts, who possess the necessary knowledge, and ontology engineers, who formalise it. This paper introduces IDEA2, a novel, semi-automated workflow that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) within a collaborative, expert-in-the-loop process to address this challenge.
